## S22/S12/S24 Executable Protected Route Smoke Harness

- Date: 2026-06-17
- Time: 01:35 HKT
- Session ID: S22
- Workstream: Production reliability and release engineering
- Status: Completed
- Objective: Add an executable signed-admin route smoke harness for the S12/S24 protected voice asset governance APIs.
- Responsible sessions: S22 Build Quality, S12 Backend/API Platform, S24 Asset and Export Quality, S19 API Configuration, S11 Regression Quality, S25 Safety Scan
- Summary of work completed:
  - Added `scripts/ai-route-smoke.mjs`.
  - Dry-run mode prints a redacted route smoke plan with S22/S19 prerequisites and the S22/S12/S24 route inventory.
  - Live mode requires `--approved`, a deployment base URL, and `UAIS_AI_ACCESS_SIGNING_SECRET`; it creates a short-lived signed admin access header for `s22-route-smoke-admin`.
  - The harness GETs `/api/ai/voice-assets/retention-readiness` and `/api/ai/voice-clone/lifecycle-audit`, then reports only route id, action, expected status, observed HTTP status, status, and responsible sessions.
  - Response bodies, signing secrets, provider keys, private cloned voice IDs, local paths, and audio payloads are never printed.

  - `npm run test -- tests/ai-env-and-smoke.test.ts -t "protected route smoke"`: passed after implementation, 3 targeted tests.

## S22/S12/S24 External Storage Reference Service

- Date: 2026-06-17
- Session ID: S22/S12/S24
- Workstream: Production reliability, backend storage, and asset governance
- Status: Completed
- Objective: Move the external durable storage blocker from contract/live-smoke harness only to a runnable, file-backed reference service that can satisfy the existing external storage smoke contract locally without using real credentials.
- Summary of work completed:
  - Added `scripts/external-storage-service.mjs`.
  - The service exposes the external storage contract:
    - `GET /teacher-ai-ownership/{teacherId}`
    - `POST /teacher-ai-ownership/{teacherId}/merge`
    - `GET /qwen-voice-lifecycle-audit`
    - `POST /qwen-voice-lifecycle-audit`

## S22 Browser Smoke NODE_PATH Runtime Support

- Date: 2026-06-18
- Time: 01:52 HKT
- Session ID: S22
- Workstream: Production E2E release browser-smoke runtime setup
- Status: Completed for NODE_PATH runtime support; overall enterprise UAIS goal remains active and incomplete.
- Objective: Provide a non-package-change execution path for the deployed browser smoke while keeping the simple transient npx failure explicit.
- Summary of work completed:
  - Confirmed `npm exec --package playwright -- node ...` and `npx --package playwright --call "node ..."` do not make `import("playwright")` resolvable.
  - Confirmed a transient npx execution can make Playwright resolvable when `NODE_PATH` is explicitly derived from the temporary Playwright CLI path and the script uses Node `require` resolution.
  - Added RED coverage with a fake `playwright` module exposed through `NODE_PATH`; the live browser smoke initially failed because dynamic ESM import did not load the NODE_PATH runtime.
  - Updated `scripts/teacher-workflow-browser-smoke.mjs` to load Playwright through `createRequire`, while keeping the existing fail-closed runtime prerequisite.
  - Updated dry-run `runtimeSetup` metadata to `moduleResolution: "node-require-resolution"` and added a redacted `transientRuntimeCommand` alongside the project-local package install path.
  - Refreshed browser smoke dry-run evidence, aggregate release-gate evidence, and the production release unblocker matrix.
- Files changed:
  - `scripts/teacher-workflow-browser-smoke.mjs`
  - `tests/teacher-workflow-browser-smoke.test.ts`
  - `coordination/reports/2026-06-17-teacher-workflow-browser-smoke-dry-run.json`
  - `coordination/reports/2026-06-17-teacher-workflow-browser-smoke-local-env-dry-run.json`
  - `coordination/reports/2026-06-17-production-e2e-release-gate.json`
  - `coordination/reports/2026-06-17-production-release-unblocker-matrix.md`
  - `coordination/session-logs/2026-06-17-S22.md`
- Checks run:
  - `npm exec --yes --package playwright -- node --input-type=module -e "... import('playwright') ..."`: failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND`, confirming the simple transient npm exec path is not enough.
  - `npx --yes --package playwright --call "node --input-type=module -e ... import('playwright') ..."`: failed with `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND`, confirming the simple transient npx call path is not enough.

## S22 Vercel Production Deployment Evidence Harness

- Date: 2026-06-18
- Time: 03:21 HKT

- Summary of work completed:
  - Added `scripts/vercel-production-deployment-evidence.mjs`.
  - The harness supports dry-run evidence, approved live deploy evidence, and approved live inspect evidence.
  - Live mode requires `--approved`.
  - Deploy/inspect requires `VERCEL_TOKEN` through the environment or approved env file; the harness explicitly rejects `--token`.
  - Live deploy calls Vercel through project-local CLI resolution when available, uses `vercel deploy --prod --yes`, captures the deployment URL only to derive a redacted deployment fingerprint and remote-HTTPS origin class, and omits stdout/stderr from evidence.
  - Live inspect blocks non-remote-HTTPS production origins before invoking Vercel.
  - Missing `VERCEL_TOKEN` blocks before invoking Vercel.
  - Updated `scripts/production-e2e-orchestrator.mjs` so the production deployment step uses the harness and the final release-gate command passes `--vercel-production-deployment <evidence>`.

  - Updated the production release unblocker matrix to remove the old raw `--token` placeholder command and document the harness.
